Your primary role is to assist users by executing commands, modifying code, and solving technical problems effectively. You should be thorough, methodical, and prioritize quality over speed.

</EFFICIENCY>

<FILE_SYSTEM_GUIDELINES>
* When a user provides a file path, do NOT assume it's relative to the current working directory. First explore the file system to locate the file before working on it.
* If asked to edit a file, edit the file directly, rather than creating a new file with a different filename.
* For global search-and-replace operations, consider using `sed` instead of opening file editors multiple times.
* NEVER create multiple versions of the same file with different suffixes (e.g., file_test.py, file_fix.py, file_simple.py). Instead:
  - Always modify the original file directly when making changes
  - If you need to create a temporary file for testing, delete it once you've confirmed your solution works
  - If you decide a file you created is no longer useful, delete it instead of creating a new version
* Do NOT include documentation files explaining your changes in version control unless the user explicitly requests it
* When reproducing bugs or implementing fixes, use a single file rather than creating multiple files with different versions
</FILE_SYSTEM_GUIDELINES>

<CODE_QUALITY>
* Write clean, efficient code with minimal comments. Avoid redundancy in comments: Do not repeat information that can be easily inferred from the code itself.
* When implementing solutions, focus on making the minimal changes needed to solve the problem.
* Before implementing any changes, first thoroughly understand the codebase through exploration.
* If you are adding a lot of code to a function or file, consider splitting the function or file into smaller pieces when appropriate.
* Place all imports at the top of the file unless explicitly requested otherwise or if placing imports at the top would cause issues (e.g., circular imports, conditional imports, or imports that need to be delayed for specific reasons).

    22	# 📦 Installation
    23	
    24	Please take a look at the documentation for different ways of installing Streamlink:
    25	
    26	- [Windows][streamlink-installation-windows]
    27	- [macOS][streamlink-installation-macos]
    28	- [Linux and BSD][streamlink-installation-linux-and-bsd]
    29	- [PyPI package and source code][streamlink-installation-pypi-source]
    30	
    31	
    32	# 👍 Features
    33	
    34	Streamlink is built on top of a plugin system which allows support for new services to be added easily.  
    35	Most of the popular streaming services are supported, such as [Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv), [YouTube](https://www.youtube.com), and many more.
    36	
    37	A list of all plugins currently included can be found on the [plugins page][streamlink-plugins].
    38	
    39	
    40	# 💡 Quickstart
    41	
    42	After installing, simply run:
    43	
    44	```sh
    45	streamlink "STREAMURL" best
    46	```
    47	
    48	The default behavior of Streamlink is to play back streams in the [VLC player][player-vlc], but a lot of other options and output methods are available, such as writing the stream to the filesystem, reading stream metadata, etc.
    49	
    50	For more in-depth usage, please refer to the [CLI documentation][streamlink-documentation-cli].
    51	
    52	An [API guide][streamlink-documentation-apiguide] and [API reference][streamlink-documentation-apiref] is available for Python implementors of Streamlink.
    53	
    54

     2	$description TikTok is a short-form video hosting service owned by ByteDance.
     3	$url www.tiktok.com
     4	$type live
     5	$metadata id

     7	$metadata title
     8	"""

    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import logging
    13	import re
    14	
    15	from streamlink.plugin import Plugin, pluginmatcher
    16	from streamlink.plugin.api import validate
    17	from streamlink.stream.http import HTTPStream
    18	
    19	
    20	log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21	
    22	
    23	@pluginmatcher(
    24	    re.compile(
    25	        r"https?://(?:www\.)?tiktok\.com/@(?P<channel>[^/?]+)",
    26	    ),
    27	)
    28	class TikTok(Plugin):
    29	    QUALITY_WEIGHTS: dict[str, int] = {}
    30	
    31	    _URL_WEB_LIVE = "https://www.tiktok.com/@{channel}/live"
    32	    _URL_API_LIVE_DETAIL = "https://www.tiktok.com/api/live/detail/?aid=1988&roomID={room_id}"
    33	    _URL_WEBCAST_ROOM_INFO = "https://webcast.tiktok.com/webcast/room/info/?aid=1988&room_id={room_id}"
    34	
    35	    _STATUS_OFFLINE = 4
    36	
    37	    @classmethod
    38	    def stream_weight(cls, key):
    39	        weight = cls.QUALITY_WEIGHTS.get(key)
    40	        if weight:
    41	            return weight, key
    42	
    43	        return super().stream_weight(key)
    44	
    45	    def _get_streams(self):
    46	        self.id = self.session.http.get(
    47	            self._URL_WEB_LIVE.format(channel=self.match["channel"]),
    48	            allow_redirects=False,
    49	            schema=validate.Schema(
    50	                validate.parse_html(),
    51	                validate.any(
    52	                    validate.all(
    53	                        validate.xml_xpath_string(
    54	                            ".//head/meta[@property='al:android:url'][contains(@content,'live?room_id=')]/@content",
    55	                        ),
    56	                        str,
    57	                        re.compile(r"room_id=(\d+)"),
    58	                        validate.get(1),
    59	                    ),
    60	                    validate.all(
    61	                        validate.xml_xpath_string(
    62	                            ".//script[@type='application/json'][@id='SIGI_STATE'][1]/text()",
    63	                        ),
    64	                        str,
    65	                        validate.parse_json(),
    66	                        {
    67	                            "LiveRoom": {
    68	                                "liveRoomUserInfo": {
    69	                                    "user": {
    70	                                        "roomId": str,
    71	                                    },
    72	                                },
    73	                            },
    74	                        },
    75	                        validate.get(("LiveRoom", "liveRoomUserInfo", "user", "roomId")),
    76	                    ),
    77	                    validate.transform(lambda *_: None),
    78	                ),
    79	            ),
    80	        )
    81	        if not self.id:
    82	            log.error("Could not find room ID")
    83	            return
    84	
    85	        log.debug(f"room_id={self.id}")
    86	
    87	        live_detail = self.session.http.get(
    88	            self._URL_API_LIVE_DETAIL.format(room_id=self.id),
    89	            schema=validate.Schema(
    90	                validate.parse_json(),
    91	                {
    92	                    "status_code": 0,
    93	                    "LiveRoomInfo": {
    94	                        "status": int,
    95	                        "title": str,
    96	                        "ownerInfo": {"nickname": str},
    97	                    },
    98	                },
    99	                validate.get("LiveRoomInfo"),
   100	                validate.union_get(
   101	                    "status",
   102	                    ("ownerInfo", "nickname"),
   103	                    "title",
   104	                ),
   105	            ),
   106	        )
   107	        status, self.author, self.title = live_detail
   108	        if status == self._STATUS_OFFLINE:
   109	            log.info("The channel is currently offline")
   110	            return
   111	
   112	        streams = self.session.http.get(
   113	            self._URL_WEBCAST_ROOM_INFO.format(room_id=self.id),
   114	            schema=validate.Schema(
   115	                validate.parse_json(),
   116	                {"data": {validate.optional("stream_url"): {"live_core_sdk_data": {"pull_data": {"stream_data": str}}}}},
   117	                validate.get(("data", "stream_url")),
   118	                validate.none_or_all(
   119	                    validate.get(("live_core_sdk_data", "pull_data", "stream_data")),
   120	                    validate.parse_json(),
   121	                    {
   122	                        "data": {
   123	                            str: validate.all(
   124	                                {
   125	                                    "main": {
   126	                                        "flv": validate.url(),
   127	                                        "sdk_params": validate.all(
   128	                                            validate.parse_json(),
   129	                                            {
   130	                                                "vbitrate": int,
   131	                                            },
   132	                                        ),
   133	                                    },
   134	                                },
   135	                                validate.union_get(
   136	                                    ("main", "flv"),
   137	                                    ("main", "sdk_params", "vbitrate"),
   138	                                ),
   139	                            ),
   140	                        },
   141	                    },
   142	                    validate.get("data"),
   143	                ),
   144	            ),
   145	        )
   146	        if not streams:
   147	            log.error("The stream is inaccessible")
   148	            return
   149	
   150	        for name, (url, vbitrate) in streams.items():
   151	            self.QUALITY_WEIGHTS[name] = vbitrate
   152	            yield name, HTTPStream(self.session, url)

   116	                validate.get(-1),
   117	                validate.none_or_all(

   119	                    validate.transform(lambda s: re.sub(r"^\w+:", "", s)),

"""
Test script to reproduce the TikTok room ID issue.
"""

import sys
import os

# Add the src directory to the path so we can import streamlink
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'src'))

from streamlink import Streamlink
from streamlink.plugin.api import validate
from streamlink.plugin.api.validate import Schema


def test_tiktok_room_extraction():
    """Test that demonstrates the TikTok room ID extraction issue."""
    
    # Create a mock session
    session = Streamlink()
    
    # Sample HTML that simulates the current TikTok page structure
    # Based on the issue description, the SIGI_STATE script has empty roomId
    sample_html_with_empty_roomid = '''
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html>
    <head>
        <meta property="al:android:url" content="snssdk1128://live?room_id=&amp;owner_id=123456789&amp;from=live"/>
        <script id="SIGI_STATE" type="application/json">
        {
            "LiveRoom": {
                "liveRoomUserInfo": {
                    "user": {
                        "roomId": "",
                        "id": "123456789",
                        "nickname": "testuser"
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        </script>
    </head>
    <body>
        <!-- TikTok live page content -->
    </body>
    </html>
    '''
    
    # Sample HTML with valid room ID (old format that worked)
    sample_html_with_valid_roomid = '''
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html>
    <head>
        <meta property="al:android:url" content="snssdk1128://live?room_id=67890123456789&amp;owner_id=123456789&amp;from=live"/>
        <script id="SIGI_STATE" type="application/json">
        {
            "LiveRoom": {
                "liveRoomUserInfo": {
                    "user": {
                        "roomId": "67890123456789",
                        "id": "123456789",
                        "nickname": "testuser"
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        </script>
    </head>
    <body>
        <!-- TikTok live page content -->
    </body>
    </html>
    '''
    
    # Test the current TikTok plugin extraction logic
    schema = validate.Schema(
        validate.parse_html(),
        validate.any(
            validate.all(
                validate.xml_xpath_string(
                    ".//head/meta[@property='al:android:url'][contains(@content,'live?room_id=')]/@content",
                ),
                str,
                validate.regex(r"room_id=(\d+)"),
                validate.get(1),
            ),
            validate.all(
                validate.xml_xpath_string(
                    ".//script[@type='application/json'][@id='SIGI_STATE'][1]/text()",
                ),
                str,
                validate.parse_json(),
                {
                    "LiveRoom": {
                        "liveRoomUserInfo": {
                            "user": {
                                "roomId": str,
                            },
                        },
                    },
                },
                validate.get(("LiveRoom", "liveRoomUserInfo", "user", "roomId")),
            ),
            validate.transform(lambda *_: None),
        ),
    )
    
    print("Testing TikTok room ID extraction...")
    
    # Test with empty room ID (current issue)
    print("\n1. Testing with empty room ID (simulating current issue):")
    try:
        result = schema.validate(sample_html_with_empty_roomid)
        print(f"   Extracted room ID: {result}")
        if result is None:
            print("   ERROR: Could not find room ID (this reproduces the issue)")
        else:
            print("   SUCCESS: Room ID found")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"   Exception: {e}")
        
    # Test with valid room ID (old working case)  
    print("\n2. Testing with valid room ID (simulating old working case):")
    try:
        result = schema.validate(sample_html_with_valid_roomid)
        print(f"   Extracted room ID: {result}")
        if result is None:
            print("   ERROR: Could not find room ID")
        else:
            print("   SUCCESS: Room ID found")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"   Exception: {e}")

    return result is not None


if __name__ == "__main__":
    success = test_tiktok_room_extraction()
    print(f"\nTest {'PASSED' if success else 'FAILED'}")
    sys.exit(0 if success else 1)</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>File created successfully at: /workspace/streamlink__streamlink__7.1/test_tiktok_issue.py</tool_response><|assistant|></think><tool_call>execute_bash<arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>cd /workspace/streamlink__streamlink__7.1 && python test_tiktok_issue.py</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>Testing TikTok room ID extraction...

## Phase 5: FIX ANALYSIS<tool_call>think<arg_key>thought</arg_key><arg_value>Now I understand the issue clearly:

**Problem Statement:**
The TikTok plugin fails to extract room IDs because TikTok has changed their webpage structure. The SIGI_STATE script that previously contained the roomId is now returning an empty string for that field.

**Location of Problem:**
In `/workspace/streamlink__streamlink__7.1/src/streamlink/plugins/tiktok.py`, lines 45-83, specifically in the `_get_streams()` method where it tries to extract the room ID using two methods:
1. Meta tag approach (line 53-59)
2. SIGI_STATE script approach (line 60-76)

Both approaches can fail - the meta tag might have an empty room_id parameter, and the SIGI_STATE script now has an empty roomId field.

**How the Test Reproduces the Issue:**
My test script shows that when the roomId is an empty string, the condition `if not self.id:` evaluates to True (since empty string is falsy in Python), causing the plugin to fail with "Could not find room ID".

**Best Practices to Consider:**
1. Need to find alternative sources of room ID data on TikTok pages
2. Should maintain backward compatibility with old working formats
3. Should handle edge cases gracefully (empty values, missing data)
4. Should not break existing functionality

**How to Fix the Problem:**
I need to find alternative ways to extract the room ID. Since the current approaches are failing, I should:

1. Research what other data sources might contain the room ID in TikTok's current page structure
2. Add additional extraction methods as fallbacks
3. Or find a different approach entirely

Looking at the Douyin plugin example, they parse JavaScript data differently using regex patterns. I should investigate if TikTok has similar patterns.

However, since I can't actually browse TikTok to see the current page structure, I'll need to think about common patterns:
- Other script elements might contain JSON data with room info
- JavaScript variables might store the room ID
- API endpoint calls in the page source might reveal the room ID
- Different meta tags or data attributes

Now let me create a more comprehensive test to verify that my fix handles the specific issue mentioned:<tool_call>str_replace_editor<arg_key>path</arg_key><arg_value>/workspace/streamlink__streamlink__7.1/test_comprehensive_tiktok.py</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>create</arg_value><arg_key>file_text</arg_key><arg_value>#!/usr/bin/env python3

"""
Comprehensive test for TikTok plugin room ID extraction fix.
Tests various scenarios including the reported issue.
"""

import sys
import os
import re

# Add the src directory to the path so we can import streamlink
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'src'))

from streamlink.plugin.api import validate
from streamlink.plugins.tiktok import TikTok


def test_plugin_room_extraction_scenarios():
    """Test various room ID extraction scenarios."""
    
    # Test case 1: Valid room ID in SIGI_STATE (old working case)
    html_with_valid_sigi_state = '''<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <script id="SIGI_STATE" type="application/json">
    {
        "LiveRoom": {
            "liveRoomUserInfo": {
                "user": {
                    "roomId": "1234567890123456789",
                    "id": "987654321",
                    "nickname": "testuser"
                }
            }
        }
    }
    </script>
</head>
<body></body>
</html>'''
    
    # Test case 2: Empty room ID in SIGI_STATE (current issue)
    html_with_empty_sigi_state = '''<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <script id="SIGI_STATE" type="application/json">
    {
        "LiveRoom": {
            "liveRoomUserInfo": {
                "user": {
                    "roomId": "",
                    "id": "987654321",
                    "nickname": "testuser"
                }
            }
        }
    }
    </script>
</head>
<body></body>
</html>'''
    
    # Test case 3: Valid room ID in meta tag (alternative method)
    html_with_valid_meta_tag = '''<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <meta property="al:android:url" content="snssdk1128://live?room_id=9876543210987654321&amp;owner_id=123456789&amp;from=live"/>
</head>
<body></body>
</html>'''
    
    # Test case 4: Empty room ID in meta tag
    html_with_empty_meta_tag = '''<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <meta property="al:android:url" content="snssdk1128://live?room_id=&amp;owner_id=123456789&amp;from=live"/>
</head>
<body></body>
</html>'''
    
    # Test case 5: No room ID available at all
    html_with_no_room_id = '''<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>TikTok Live</title>
</head>
<body></body>
</html>'''
    
    # Create plugin instance for testing
    plugin = TikTok()
    
    print("Testing TikTok room ID extraction scenarios...")
    
    # Test the schema directly
    schema = validate.Schema(
        validate.parse_html(),
        validate.any(
            validate.all(
                validate.xml_xpath_string(
                    ".//head/meta[@property='al:android:url'][contains(@content,'live?room_id=')]/@content",
                ),
                str,
                validate.regex(re.compile(r"room_id=(\d*)")),  # Allow empty capture group
                validate.get(1),
                # Ensure the extracted room ID is not empty
                validate.transform(lambda x: x if x else None),
            ),
            validate.all(
                validate.xml_xpath_string(
                    ".//script[@type='application/json'][@id='SIGI_STATE'][1]/text()",
                ),
                str,
                validate.parse_json(),
                {
                    "LiveRoom": {
                        "liveRoomUserInfo": {
                            "user": {
                                "roomId": str,
                            },
                        },
                    },
                },
                validate.get(("LiveRoom", "liveRoomUserInfo", "user", "roomId")),
                # Ensure the extracted room ID is not empty
                validate.transform(lambda x: x if x else None),
            ),
            validate.transform(lambda *_: None),
        ),
    )
    
    results = []
    
    test_cases = [
        ("Valid SIGI_STATE room ID", html_with_valid_sigi_state, "1234567890123456789"),
        ("Empty SIGI_STATE room ID", html_with_empty_sigi_state, None),
        ("Valid meta tag room ID", html_with_valid_meta_tag, "9876543210987654321"),
        ("Empty meta tag room ID", html_with_empty_meta_tag, None),
        ("No room ID available", html_with_no_room_id, None),
    ]
    
    for test_name, html_content, expected in test_cases:
        try:
            result = schema.validate(html_content)
            success = (result == expected)
            results.append(success)
            status = "PASS" if success else "FAIL"
            print(f"  {test_name}: {status} (got: {result}, expected: {expected})")
        except Exception as e:
            results.append(False)
            print(f"  {test_name}: ERROR - {e}")
    
    # Overall result
    passed = sum(results)
    total = len(results)
    print(f"\nResults: {passed}/{total} tests passed")
    
    return passed == total


if __name__ == "__main__":
    success = test_plugin_room_extraction_scenarios()
    print(f"\nOverall test {'PASSED' if success else 'FAILED'}")
    sys.exit(0 if success else 1)</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>File created successfully at: /workspace/streamlink__streamlink__7.1/test_comprehensive_tiktok.py</tool_response><|assistant|></think><tool_call>execute_bash<arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>cd /workspace/streamlink__streamlink__7.1 && python test_comprehensive_tiktok.py</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>Traceback (most recent call last):

</html>'''
    
    print("Testing TikTok room ID extraction scenarios...")
    
    # Test the schema directly
    schema = validate.Schema(
        validate.parse_html(),
        validate.any(
            validate.all(
                validate.xml_xpath_string(
                    ".//head/meta[@property='al:android:url'][contains(@content,'live?room_id=')]/@content",
                ),
                str,
                validate.regex(re.compile(r"room_id=(\d*)")),  # Allow empty capture group
                validate.get(1),
                # Ensure the extracted room ID is not empty
                validate.transform(lambda x: x if x else None),
            ),
            validate.all(
                validate.xml_xpath_string(
                    ".//script[@type='application/json'][@id='SIGI_STATE'][1]/text()",
                ),
                str,
                validate.parse_json(),
                {
                    "LiveRoom": {
                        "liveRoomUserInfo": {
                            "user": {
                                "roomId": str,
                            },
                        },
                    },
                },
                validate.get(("LiveRoom", "liveRoomUserInfo", "user", "roomId")),
                # Ensure the extracted room ID is not empty
                validate.transform(lambda x: x if x else None),
            ),
            validate.transform(lambda *_: None),
        ),
    )
    
    results = []
    
    test_cases = [
        ("Valid SIGI_STATE room ID", html_with_valid_sigi_state, "1234567890123456789"),
        ("Empty SIGI_STATE room ID", html_with_empty_sigi_state, None),
        ("Valid meta tag room ID", html_with_valid_meta_tag, "9876543210987654321"),
        ("Empty meta tag room ID", html_with_empty_meta_tag, None),
        ("No room ID available", html_with_no_room_id, None),
    ]
    
    for test_name, html_content, expected in test_cases:
        try:
            result = schema.validate(html_content)
            success = (result == expected)
            results.append(success)
            status = "PASS" if success else "FAIL"
            print(f"  {test_name}: {status} (got: {result}, expected: {expected})")
        except Exception as e:
            results.append(False)
            print(f"  {test_name}: ERROR - {e}")
    
    # Overall result
    passed = sum(results)
    total = len(results)
    print(f"\nResults: {passed}/{total} tests passed")
    
    return passed == total


if __name__ == "__main__":
    success = test_plugin_room_extraction_scenarios()
    print(f"\nOverall test {'PASSED' if success else 'FAILED'}")
    sys.exit(0 if success else 1)</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>File created successfully at: /workspace/streamlink__streamlink__7.1/test_comprehensive_tiktok.py</tool_response><|assistant|></think><tool_call>execute_bash<arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>cd /workspace/streamlink__streamlink__7.1 && python test_comprehensive_tiktok.py</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>Testing TikTok room ID extraction scenarios...
